Consider continuous optimization discretized: Let n be e.g. 200-2000. Variables a_i≥0, sum=1. Need minimize max b. This is a smooth (nonconvex) max-of-quadratics. We can use algorithms:
1. Start from good parametric priors: decreasing linear, quadratic, exponential, two-sided, etc.
2. Use multiplicative gradient descent / projected subgradient:
Given a>0 sum1. Compute b = conv(a,a). Find active indices K = where b close to max (top few). Subgradient g = average over active k of ∂b_k/∂a. For each active k, ∂b_k/∂a_i = ∑_{j: i+j=k} a_j. This can be computed via convolution of reversed a? Let's derive: For fixed k, gradient vector v_i(a) = a_{k-i} if 0≤k-i<n else 0, but if i = k-i (when k even and i=k/2), derivative of a_i^2 is 2a_i? Actually b_k =∑_{i} a_i a_{k-i}. If i≠k-i, terms appear twice? In summation over all i, derivative w.r.t a_i includes terms i and k-i. If i≠k-i: derivative = a_{k-i}+a_i? Let's check b_k =∑_i a_i a_{k-i}. If i and k-i both in range, terms: i term a_i a_{k-i}; k-i term a_{k-i} a_i. Both depend on a_i. Derivative = a_{k-i}+a_{k-i}? Wait derivative of a_i a_{k-i} wrt a_i = a_{k-i}. Derivative of a_{k-i} a_i wrt a_i = a_{k-i}. So total 2a_{k-i} (unless i=k-i, derivative of a_i^2 =2a_i). In general ∂b_k/∂a_i = 2 a_{k-i} if 0≤k-i<n and i≠k-i? If i=k-i, it's 2a_i consistent. So gradient = 2 * (reversed a)_i where (a^R)_i = a_{k-i}. More precisely v_i = 2 a_{k-i} if k-i in range else 0. For all k, gradient vector = 2 r_k where r_k is slice of a at indices k-i; across k.
If using subgradient for active set equally weighted, g_i = 2/|K| ∑_{k∈active, 0≤k-i<n} a_{k-i}. This is O(|K|n) or can compute via convolution of mask of active with a? Specifically for each i, sum active b_k/k-i? Active mask m_k, g_i =2 (m * a^rev?) can compute convolution of active indicator length2n-1 with a? Since n moderate, O(n^2) maybe okay for n up to few thousand if few iterations? We can simplify: subgradient for single active k is 2 a reversed at offset k. Multiplicative update: a_i *= exp(-η g_i) then normalize. This tends to reduce active bins.
But max objective nonconvex; simple gradient may work.

Alternative: Use the LP iteration from state of art. For a given a and M=max b, solve max sum g s.t. a*g ≤ M i.e. for each k, ∑_i a_i g_{k-i} ≤ M. Normalize. What about polynomial shapes with support entire, like a_i = (i+1)^p (increasing) but decreasing maybe bad. Maybe pairs can be balanced by a U-shaped asymmetric? Let's think of small n optimization via actual code will be needed.

Given we can run optimization at runtime for many seconds, we can likely find a sequence below 1.5. Need design search powerful enough.

Potential algorithms:
1. Use scipy.optimize.minimize for n=10-30 with log-sum-exp objective, many random restarts. Since target existence 1.5027, probably moderate n optimum can be found.
2. Use multiplicative subgradient for medium n (up to 500) to approach continuous optimum if it exists.
3. Use parameterized families with side scans and optimize parameters (e.g., polynomial of degree d on [0,n-1] with positive coefficients). We can search dense continuous densities using polynomial basis and minimize sup objective. This may find better than random.

Let's formulate a dense continuous density approximation. Choose n (e.g., 300-2000), let a_i be a positive polynomial P(i/n) (or piecewise) normalized. We can optimize coefficients. Given runtime, we can implement a continuous density optimizer using grid and multiplicative updates. It should find good shapes.

Let's design a fast objective evaluator for dense arrays:
- Normalize sum=1 internally for optimization; evaluate_sequence can be called on scaled list to get official score.
- Use FFT convolution to compute b quickly for n up to e.g. 4096. Implement fft_convolve(a,a): N = next power of two >=2n-1; F=fft(a, N); b = ifft(F*F).real. For real nonnegative, small negative set0. This is O(N log N).
- We can compute smooth gradient as above with FFT of active weights: ar=a[::-1]; w weights length 2n-1; conv=fftconvolve(ar,w); g=conv[n-1:n-1+n]*2.
Use scipy.signal.fftconvolve? Available? scipy allowed. But to avoid overhead, implement with numpy FFT:
def fft_convolve(x,y): N=1 << (len(x)+len(y)-1).bit_length(); fx=np.fft.rfft(x,N); fy=np.fft.rfft(y,N); return np.fft.irfft(fx*fy,N)[:len(x)+len(y)-1]
For square convolve a,a, use same.
This may be faster than np.convolve for n>1000.

Optimization procedure for fixed n:
- Define smooth objective J(a)=η * log(∑ exp(β b_k)) maybe plus? But we can use gradient iterations directly, not scipy.
- Multiplicative log-sum-exp gradient:
  1. a positive sum=1.
  2. b=fftconv(a,a)
  3. β = 20 / max(b,1e-12) maybe; weights = exp(β*(b-M)); normalize.
  4. g = 2 corr(w,a) as above.
  5. Normalize gradient: g -= np.mean(g) (or weighted mean?) to reduce sum. Since a sum1, changing a by exp(-ηg) changes sum; normalize anyway.
  6. a *= exp(-η * g_scale); clip; normalize.
  7. Rescale official? official score =2n*max(b) (since sum1). Keep best max.
Tune η, β. This is like minimizing smooth maximum. Starting from random or parametric shapes.
However b quadratics not convex; gradient of logsumexp of b is exact for smooth abs, may work.

Let's detail smooth optimizer:
Function softmax(x): z=x-max(x); e=np.exp(z); return e/sum(e).
Function convolve_fft(a): N next power of two >=2*n-1. Could cache N. Return real.
For small n, np.convolve might be faster and exact; use np.convolve for n≤512? np.convolve in C direct O(n^2) for 512 ~0.5M, fine. For n up to 2048, use FFT.
function convolution(a): if n<=512: return np.convolve(a,a); else fft.
function gradient_from_weights(a,w): compute ar = a[::-1]; if n<=512: conv=np.convolve(ar,w); else fft; return 2*conv[n-1:n-1+n]. Need w length 2n-1.
Smooth objective and gradient for u:
```
def smooth_objective_and_grad(u, beta, return_grad=True):
    a=softmax(u)
    b=conv(a,a)
    # logsumexp stable
    m=max(b)
    s=np.sum(np.exp(beta*(b-m)))
    F = m + np.log(s)/beta  # equals 1/beta logsumexp
    w=np.exp(beta*(b-m))/s
    ga = gradient_from_weights(a,w) # this is sum w * gradient b (no beta)
    if return_grad:
        grad = a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
        # optional scaling / Hessian approx? return F, grad
    return (F,grad) or F
```
This is the gradient of F w.r.t u. But F approximates max b. The scale of F ~b~1/n. If beta large, F≈max. The gradient magnitude ~a*ga, ga~b? Actually ga dimension b? Sum w∂b ~ M? ∂b terms ~a, w sum1, ga ~average a over active ~1/n. So grad ~1/n^2. L-BFGS handles.
We can use scipy.optimize.minimize(method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':2000,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8}). But u has flat direction (adding constant). We can project gradient by subtracting mean (done). Also re-center u before each eval? We can inside objective set u0 = u - np.mean(u) to stabilize; but if we modify x, gradient same. In optimization, we can pre-center initial and after each? The objective is invariant to u shift; L-BFGS may have issues. We can define a wrapper that centers u: u0=u-np.mean(u); but then derivative of object w.r.t original u? Since centering is linear with nullspace constant, gradient w.r.t original = gradient w.r.t u0 (because derivative of u0 w.r.t u = I - 11^T/n, and gradient in u0 already orthogonal to 1, so same). We can simply enforce u = u - mean(u) at start of objective and return gradient (which is orthogonal). L-BFGS should be okay.
But calling minimize with custom jac will evaluate many times. For n=10, maxiter 1000 fine. For n=80, maybe.
We can also use Nelder-Mead? no.

LP iteration details:
```
def lp_iteration(a, M, maxlf=1):
   n=len(a); row=2*n-1
   # constraints matrix A: row k, col j = a[k-j] if 0<=k-j<n
   # Build dense: A = np.zeros((2n-1,n)); for j in range(n): A[j:j+2n-1? Actually row k with fixed j: k=j+i? If variable g_j, appears in constraints k=j+i for i=0..n-1 => rows j..j+n-1 with coefficient a_i.
   A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   for j in range(n):
       A[j:j+n, j] = a  # because i=0..n-1, k=j+i, coeff a_i? Constraint row k: sum_i a_i g_{k-i}. Let j=k-i => i=k-j; coeff a_{k-j}. For j fixed, rows k=j+i (i=0..n-1), coeff a_i. Yes.
   c = -np.ones(n) (minimize -sum)
   bounds [(0,None)]*n
   res=linprog(c,A,b=M*ones, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
   if success: g=res.x; G=sum(g); if G>0: u=a.sum()/G*g; else u=a
   return u
```
This matrix can be built efficiently via diagonals: For each j, diagonal from j to j+n-1 with a. Use scipy.sparse.diags? But dense for n≤50 okay. Use arrays:
```
A=np.zeros((2n-1,n), dtype=float)
for j,a in enumerate(A.T): ...
```

Line search update:
```
def combine(a,u,steps):
   best_score=score(a); best=a
   for t in steps:
      f=(1-t)*a+t*u; f=np.clip(f,0,None); if sum small; score=score_normalized(f) (2n*max(conv)); if score<best_score update.
   return f,score
```
Since sum of u same as a, normalization okay.
We can allow u to change support; if u has zeros, a remains positive if t<1.
M = max conv(a). If LP G <= sum(a) * (1-1e-6), no improvement; stop.

Let's write a simple "multiplicative_smooth" optimizer for medium n without scipy:
```
def multiplicative_update(a, beta, eta, iterations):
  for it in range(iterations):
    b=conv(a,a); M=max(b)
    w=softmax(beta*(b-M)) (sum not needed for weights? use divide sum)
    ga=gradient_from_weights(a,w)
    ga -= np.mean(ga)
    a *= np.exp(-eta * ga / (M+1e-12))
    a=np.clip(a, floor, None); a/sum
```
Step eta maybe between0.1 and1. This is gradient descent on log-sum-exp. Might be stable and fast. We can run for n up to 2000 easily. Use adaptive eta: try eta=0.5, if score not improve maybe eta*=1.5? Since we can compute score after. But smooth gradient with fixed eta may need many iterations. Use L-BFGS for small n, multiplicative for large.

We can implement a function `run_multiplicative(a, beta_start, eta, max_iter, deadline, report)`:
- a normalized sum1 positive.
- For stage? Maybe use beta = 20*n / max(b) dynamically? But if beta changes, it's not a fixed objective; heuristic okay. Use dynamic beta = c / M to always concentrate top active. For subgradient of max, choose active weights softmax(c*(b-M)), c maybe 10-100. Dynamic stable.
- Update with eta = 0.5 / (np.max(|ga|)+eps) maybe. Since ga scale ~M? b scale M; gradient scale ~? Use ga normalized by M: step = np.exp(-eta * ga / M). If ga/M ~O(1), eta 0.1-1. Try eta=0.5.
- To avoid oscillation, combine with previous.
Pseudo:
```
for it:
  b=conv(a,a); M=max(b)
  w=softmax(20*(b-M)/M? actually 20*(b-M) with M scale -> dimensionless 20*(b/M -1))
  ga=grad(a,w)
  ga=(ga - np.mean(ga))/(M+eps)
  for eta in [0.5,1.0,0.25]:
      cand = a*np.exp(-eta*ga); normalize; score; if improve accept and break
  if no improve over 500 updates: break
```
This is a heuristic but may reduce score.

```
def get_official_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e300)
    mx=np.max(a)
    if mx<=0: return np.inf
    scale=100.0/mx
    s=a*scale
    if np.sum(s)<0.01: scale=sum? scale=1.0/np.sum(a)
    return evaluate_sequence(s.tolist())
```

Convolution helper with FFT:
```
def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    if n <= 512:
       return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fb=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
    return fb[:2*n-1]
```
For small n, use np.convolve. Clip negative tiny.

Gradient from weights:
```
def grad_from_weights(a,w):
   n=len(a)
   ar=a[::-1]
   if n<=512:
       c=np.convolve(ar,w)
   else:
       N=1
       need=len(ar)+len(w)-1
       while N<need: N*=2
       fr=np.fft.rfft(ar,N); fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fr*fw,N)
   return 2.0 * c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Check indices: ar length n, w length2n-1. Convolution length 3n-2. c[n-1:i+n-1+n] length n. Good.

```
def smooth_obj_grad(u, beta):
   n=len(u)
   u=u-np.mean(u)
   a=np.exp(u); a=a/a.sum()
   b=conv_self(a)
   m=np.max(b)
   # logsumexp: z=beta*(b-m); s=sum(exp(z)); F=m + log(s)/beta
   # weights=exp(z)/s
   # ga=grad_from_weights(a, weights)
   ga=grad_from_weights(a, weights)
   g=a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
   return F, g
```

Multiplicative update run:
```
def run_multiplicative(a, stages=None, max_iter=5000, deadline=0):
    n=len(a)
    best=a; best_score=internal_score(a)
    c=20.0  # beta factor
    eta=0.5
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       b=conv_self(a); M=max(b)
       # weights dynamic beta=c/M to get c*(b/M-1)
       z=c*(b/M -1.0); z-=max(z); w=exp(z); w/=sum(w)
       ga=grad_from_weights(a,w)
       ga=ga-np.mean(ga)
       scale=M+1e-300
       # try eta candidates for robustness
       improved=False
       for e in (eta, eta*2, eta*0.5):
           cand=a*np.exp(-e*ga/scale)
           cand=np.clip(cand,1e-15,None)
           cand/=cand.sum()
           sc=internal_score(cand)
           if sc<best_score-1e-12:
               best=cand; best_score=sc; a=cand; improved=True; break
       if improved:
           stall=0
       else:
           eta=max(0.05, eta*0.7)
           stall+=1
           if stall>200: break
    return a,best_score
```
This updates only if improves actual score (not smooth). It may be slow if eta too small; try eta values. We can also accept small smooth decrease? But using actual score conservative improves.
Dynamic beta c maybe from 10 to100. We can schedule c: [10,20,40,80]. For each stage reset eta=0.5? Use loop.

We can compute candidate score by evaluating `2*len(cand)*max(conv_self(cand))`.

LP iteration function:
```
def lp_update(a, M):
   n=len(a)
   # Build A dense
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   for j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j]=a
   c=-np.ones(n)
   res=linprog(c, A=A, b=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
   if res.success: return res.x
   return None
```
For n>80 dense big; restrict.
`run_lp(a, deadline, max_iters=200)`:
- a normalized positive.
- best=a,best_score.
- for it:
   M=max(conv_self(a)); G=sum(a)
   u=lp_update(a,M)
   if None or sum(u)<=G*(1+1e-6): break
   u=(G/sum(u))*u
   # line search over t
   candidates steps [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0] maybe adaptive.
   pick best score.
   if no improve over several: break.
Return best.

```

```

`normalize_pos(a, floor=1e-12)`:
```

```

`internal_score(a)`:


`run_de`:

```
def run_de(n, seed, deadline, maxiter=1000):
    global _DE_N
    _DE_N=n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    popsize = 20 + 3*n
    res = optimize.differential_evolution(de_objective, bounds, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=popsize, tol=1e-8, seed=seed, polish=False, disp=False, workers=1, init='sobol'?)
```
`init='sobol'` may not be available? Use 'latinhypercube' default. To be safe, don't specify init. Some scipy versions require `workers` keyword okay. If DE fails, catch.

`run_lp`: Need import `optimize.linprog`. Build A. For n > maybe 60, skip. Use options `{'presolve': True, 'time_limit': ...}`? HiGHS may accept time_limit? To be safe not. We check time between iterations.
```
def run_lp(a, deadline, max_iter=100):
    n=len(a); best=a; best_score=_internal_score(a)
    eta=...; fail=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        b=_conv_self(a); M=np.max(b)
        A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=float)
        for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=a
        c=-np.ones(n)
        try:
           res=optimize.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs')
        except Exception: break
        if not res.success or res.x is None: break
        u=res.x; G=sum(u)
        S=sum(a)
        if not finite or G<=S*(1+1e-6): break
        u=(S/G)*u
        # line search
        improved=False
        for t in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.75,0.15,0.3,1.0):
           f=(1-t)*a + t*u
           f=np.clip(f,0,None); s=f.sum(); if s==0 break; f=f/s
           sc=_internal_score(f)
           if sc<best_score:
              best=f; best_score=sc; a=f; improved=True; break
        if not improved: fail+=1
        else: fail=0
        if fail>20: break
    return best,best_score
```
Constraints b vector: `b_ub` parameter name conflicts with b convolution; use `rhs`.
`A_ub=A`.
If M is very small, linprog may have numerical; okay.

`run_multiplicative` details:
```
def run_multiplicative(a, deadline, stages=(10,20,40,80), max_iter=100000):
    a=_normalize_pos(a); best=a; best_score=_internal_score(a)
    for beta_factor in stages:
       eta=0.5
       stall=0
       for it in range(max_iter):
          if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
          b=_conv_self(a); M=float(np.max(b))
          if not finite or M<=0: break
          z=beta_factor*(b/M -1.0)
          z-=np.max(z)
          w=np.exp(z); sw=w.sum(); if sw<=0: break; w/=sw
          ga=_grad_weights(a,w)
          ga=ga-np.mean(ga)
          scale=M+1e-300
          improved=False
          for e in (eta, eta*2.0, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
              cand=a*np.exp(-e*ga/scale)
              cand=np.clip(cand,1e-15,None)
              sc=sum(cand)
              if sc<=0: continue
              cand/=sc
              sc2=_internal_score(cand)
              if sc2<best_score-1e-12:
                  a=cand; best=a.copy(); best_score=sc2; improved=True; break
          if improved: stall=0
          else:
             eta *= 0.7
             if eta<0.02: break
             stall +=1
             if stall>200: break
    return best,best_score
```

```python
def _softmax(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float)
    if u.size == 0: return u
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.ones(u.size)/u.size
    u = u - np.max(u)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(u.size)/u.size
    return e/s
...
```

`_conv_self` uses `np.fft.rfft`; need if N maybe 1. For n=1, direct.

```
def _conv_self(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    if n <= 512:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       N=1
       need=2*n-1
       while N<need: N <<=1
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
       c=c[:need]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0  # this modifies copy? c from convolve is new; okay. But if all negative? set 0.
    return c
```
If c has negative due FFT, setting all negative zero may hide small; okay. For direct, no negative.

`_grad_weights` similar; `c[np.maximum?]` but gradient can be negative? true nonnegative. Use `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`.

`_internal_score`:
```
def _internal_score(a):
    a = _normalize_pos(a)
    b = _conv_self(a)
    m = np.max(b)
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)
```

`_normalize_pos` floor:
```
def _normalize_pos(a, floor=1e-15):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).copy()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a>0), a, 0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 0 or not finite: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
   a /= s
   a = np.maximum(a, floor)
   s=a.sum(); a/=s
   return a
```
If a length 0? return empty.

`run_smooth_optimize` uses `_SMOOTH_BETA` global. Need reset after? Not critical. Use `optimize.minimize` with `bounds`. For n could be large (64), bounds length64. `maxiter_stage` maybe int.
```
def run_smooth_optimize(u, betas, deadline, maxiter_stage=100):
    u=np.asarray(u,float).copy()
    best_a=_softmax(u); best_score=_internal_score(best_a)
    bounds=[(-50.0,50.0)]*u.size
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       _SMOOTH_BETA=float(beta)
       try:
           res=optimize.minimize(_smooth_obj_func, u, jac=_smooth_jac_func, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter_stage, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':10})
       except Exception:
           break
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           a=_softmax(res.x); sc=_internal_score(a)
           if sc<best_score:
               best_score=sc; best_a=a
           u=res.x
    return best_a,best_score
```

`make_starts`: for negative power p at x=0, `(x + 1e-6)**p` might overflow for p=-2 at 1e-6 ->1e12, but normalize handles finite? 1e12 finite. For p=-3? we use -2 max. Good.

`_conv_self` for n=4498 direct threshold >512 uses FFT; for n=512 direct O(0.5M) fine. For gradient weight length w=2n-1 with ar length n, if n>512, need N >= len(ar)+len(w)-1 = (n)+(2n-1)-1=3n-2. In `_grad_weights`, if n>512, use N while N < len(ar)+len(w)-1. For n=4498, need3*4498-2=13492, N=16384. Good.
For n<=512, `np.convolve(ar,w)` length 3n-2, slice n-1:n-1+n. Good.

`_grad_weights` gradient formula check again: We want ga_i=2∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. Let ar[k]=a[n-1-k]. conv=ar*w. conv[n-1+i] =∑_{k} ar[k] w[n-1+i-k]. Let k=n-1-j => ar=a_j, index w[n-1+i-n+1+j]=w[i+j]. Good. Yes.

Smooth gradient: `ga = 2 corr` as above. Then `g = a*(ga - dot(a,ga))`. For softmax, derivative of a_j wrt u_i = a_i(δ_{ji}-a_j). `∑_j ∂F/∂a_j ∂a_j/∂u_i = a_i(∂F/∂a_i - ∑_j a_j∂F/∂a_j)`. Good.

`_smooth_compute`: We need beta parameter. For numerical, z = beta*(b-m). If beta is large and b-m negative large, exp underflows; fine. `s=sum(exp(z))`. weights=exp(z)/s. `ga = grad_weights(a, weights)`. F=m+log(s)/beta. If beta very large, F≈m. Good.

Potential issue: `_smooth_obj_func` and `_smooth_jac_func` both call `_smooth_compute`, each would recompute separately if scipy calls both separately? `minimize` with jac calls `fun` and `jac` separately? It may call `fun` first then `jac`, duplicating conv. Could be inefficient. We can make `jac` compute gradient and maybe fun not called? L-BFGS-B with `jac` calls fun separately? It likely calls both. We can store last? Use global cache? Simpler accept duplicate; n small. Or return gradient from same computation by storing in global `_SMOOTH_LAST`? Could optimize but not necessary. For large n, duplicate expensive. We can implement a top-level global `_SMOOTH_CACHE = None`. In `_smooth_obj_func`, compute and store F, maybe gradient not; in `_smooth_jac_func`, if cache for same u? Not easy. L-BFGS calls fun and jac with same x; we can in `_smooth_obj_func` store (u copy, F, ga?) and jac use if match. But no closures. Let's implement simple:
```
_SMOOTH_CACHE = None
def _smooth_obj_func(u):
   global _SMOOTH_CACHE
   F,g=_smooth_compute(u,_SMOOTH_BETA,True)
   _SMOOTH_CACHE=(u.copy(), F, g)
   return F
def _smooth_jac_func(u):
   global _SMOOTH_CACHE
   if _SMOOTH_CACHE is not None:
       u0,F,g= _SMOOTH_CACHE
       # Check equal shape and allclose? Could be expensive; use np.array_equal? u may same object? scipy passes same array? Not guaranteed. Use if np.array_equal(u0,u): return g
       if u.size==u0.size and np.array_equal(u,u0): return g
   F,g=...; return g
```
This avoids duplicate in common case. But if not equal, compute. Use `np.array_equal` O(n) okay.
Need update cache in compute? We'll do.
But `_smooth_compute` with `need_grad=False` for fun not enough; fun can compute grad for cache. Use it.

`run_smooth_optimize`: after each stage, maybe apply `_smooth_obj_func`? not needed.

`run_multiplicative`: It computes candidate score with `_internal_score`, which normalizes and convolves. We already have current b/M but candidate different. okay.

`run_lp`: building A loop may be slow for n=24 but okay. Use `for j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j] = a`. This uses vector.

Now, let's think if the final code may exceed memory due `A` dense for n=50? We skip n≤24. Good.

One issue: `evaluate_sequence` may not be defined in the runtime before propose? It says assume imported. We use at end; if NameError, catch and ignore? We can try, except NameError. But internal best valid.

Let's write final code now. Ensure final only code block.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import scipy.optimize as optimize

_DE_N = 0
_SMOOTH_BETA = 1.0
_SMOOTH_CACHE = None


def _softmax(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float)
    if u.size == 0:
        return u
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.ones(u.size, dtype=float) / float(u.size)
    u = u - np.max(u)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(u.size, dtype=float) / float(u.size)
    return e / s


def _normalize_pos(a, floor=1e-15):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=float) / float(a.size)
    a /= s
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=float) / float(a.size)
    a /= s
    return a


def _conv_self(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=float)

    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        need = 2 * n - 1
        N = 1
        while N < need:
            N <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
        c = c[:need]

    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return c


def _internal_score(a):
    a = _normalize_pos(a)
    b = _conv_self(a)
    m = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def _grad_weights(a, w):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or w.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    ar = a[::-1]

    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(ar, w)
    else:
        need = len(ar) + len(w) - 1
        N = 1
        while N < need:
            N <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)[:need]

    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def _official_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a >= 0.0), a, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(a))
    if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    scale = 100.0 / m
    s = a * scale
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(s)) or np.sum(s) < 0.01:
        scale = max(scale, 1.0 / float(np.sum(a)))
        s = a * scale
    if np.max(s) > 1000.0:
        s = s * (1000.0 / float(np.max(s)))
    return float(evaluate_sequence(s.tolist()))


def _smooth_compute(u, beta, need_grad=True):
    beta = float(beta)
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float)
    n = u.size
    if n == 0:
        if need_grad:
            return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
        return 0.0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        if need_grad:
            return 1e6, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        return 1e6

    a = _softmax(u)
    b = _conv_self(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        if need_grad:
            return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        return 0.0

    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        if need_grad:
            return 1e6, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        return 1e6

    if beta <= 0.0:
        if need_grad:
            zero = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            return m, zero
        return m

    z = beta * (b - m)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        if need_grad:
            return 1e6, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        return 1e6

    w = e / s
    F = m + float(np.log(s)) / beta

    if not need_grad:
        return F

    ga = _grad_weights(a, w)
    ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)
    g = a * (ga - float(np.dot(a, ga)))
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return F, g


def _smooth_obj_func(u):
    global _SMOOTH_CACHE
    F, g = _smooth_compute(u, _SMOOTH_BETA, True)
    _SMOOTH_CACHE = (np.asarray(u, dtype=float).copy(), F, g)
    return float(F)


def _smooth_jac_func(u):
    global _SMOOTH_CACHE
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float)
    if _SMOOTH_CACHE is not None:
        u0, F0, g0 = _SMOOTH_CACHE
        if u.size == u0.size and np.array_equal(u, u0):
            return g0
    F, g = _smooth_compute(u, _SMOOTH_BETA, True)
    return g


def run_smooth_optimize(u, betas, deadline, maxiter_stage=100):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float).copy()
    if u.size == 0:
        return u, np.inf

    best_a = _softmax(u)
    best_score = _internal_score(best_a)
    bounds = [(-50.0, 50.0)] * u.size

    for beta in betas:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        _SMOOTH_BETA = float(beta)
        try:
            res = optimize.minimize(
                _smooth_obj_func,
                u,
                jac=_smooth_jac_func,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter_stage),
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            if x.size == u.size and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                a = _softmax(x)
                sc = _internal_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a
                u = x
    return best_a, best_score


def run_multiplicative(a, deadline, stages=(10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 80.0), max_iter=2000):
    a = _normalize_pos(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _internal_score(a)

    for beta_factor in stages:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        eta = 0.5
        stall = 0
        beta_factor = float(beta_factor)

        for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= deadline:
                break

            b = _conv_self(a)
            M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                break

            z = beta_factor * (b / M - 1.0)
            z = z - np.max(z)
            e = np.exp(z)
            s = float(np.sum(e))
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                break
            w = e / s

            ga = _grad_weights(a, w)
            ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)
            ga = ga - float(np.mean(ga))
            scale = M + 1e-300

            improved = False
            for eps in (eta, 1.8 * eta, 0.55 * eta, 0.25 * eta):
                cand = a * np.exp(-eps * ga / scale)
                cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand) & (cand > 0.0), cand, 0.0)
                cs = float(np.sum(cand))
                if cs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cs):
                    continue
                cand /= cs
                sc = _internal_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                    a = cand
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    improved = True
                    break

            if improved:
                stall = 0
            else:
                eta *= 0.7
                stall += 1
                if eta < 0.02 or stall > 250:
                    break

    return best_a, best_score


def de_objective(x):
    if _DE_N <= 0:
        return 1e6
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    a = _normalize_pos(a)
    return _internal_score(a)


def run_de(n, seed, maxiter=500):
    global _DE_N
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    _DE_N = int(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    popsize = min(80, 18 + 3 * int(n))
    try:
        res = optimize.differential_evolution(
            de_objective,
            bounds,
            maxiter=int(maxiter),
            popsize=popsize,
            tol=1e-8,
            seed=int(seed),
            polish=False,
            disp=False,
            workers=1,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is None or res.x is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    if a.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    return _normalize_pos(a)


def run_lp(a, deadline, max_iter=80):
    a = _normalize_pos(np.maximum(a, 1e-8))
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return a, np.inf

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _internal_score(a)
    S = float(np.sum(best_a))

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        b = _conv_self(a)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=float)
        for j in range(n):
            A[j:j + n, j] = a

        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        rhs = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=float)
        try:
            res = optimize.linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=rhs,
                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None:
            break
        u = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if u.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
            break

        G = float(np.sum(u))
        if G <= S * (1.0 + 1e-7) or not np.isfinite(G):
            break

        u = (S / G) * u
        improved = False
        for t in (0.45, 0.2, 0.08, 0.75, 0.12, 0.32, 1.0):
            f = (1.0 - t) * a + t * u
            f = _normalize_pos(f)
            sc = _internal_score(f)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                a = f
                best_a = f.copy()
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def make_starts(n, rng, limit=24):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    starts = []

    starts.append(_normalize_pos(np.ones(n, dtype=float)))

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.5, 2.5):
        if len(starts) >= limit:
            break
        starts.append(_normalize_pos(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, float(alpha)))))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=float)

    for rate in (-4.0, -2.0, -1.2, -0.7, -0.35, 0.35, 0.7, 1.2, 2.0, 4.0):
        if len(starts) >= limit:
            break
        starts.append(_normalize_pos(np.exp(rate * x)))

    for p in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        if len(starts) >= limit:
            break
        starts.append(_normalize_pos((x + 1e-6) ** p))

    for mu in (0.15, 0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.70):
        for sig in (0.12, 0.22, 0.34, 0.52):
            if len(starts) >= limit:
                break
            starts.append(_normalize_pos(np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2) + 1e-6))

    for _ in range(5):
        if len(starts) >= limit:
            break
        noise = rng.normal(1.0, 0.9, size=n)
        noise = np.abs(noise)
        noise = 0.5 * noise + 0.5 * noise[::-1]
        starts.append(_normalize_pos(noise))

    return starts[:int(limit)]


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 0:
        budget_s = 600.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s - 12.0)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def not_after(deadline_t):
        return time.monotonic() < deadline_t

    # Use the supplied state-of-the-art sequence if it is available.
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
        if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
            p = None
        if p is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.where(p >= 0.0, p, 0.0)
            sc = _internal_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_seq = _normalize_pos(p).copy()
                print(f"[init] n={p.size} internal_score={best_score:.8f}")

    def update_best(a, tag=""):
        nonlocal best_score, best_seq
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
        if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        a = _normalize_pos(a)
        sc = _internal_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = a.copy()
            print(f"[{tag}] n={a.size} score={best_score:.8f}")

    n_list = [5, 6, 7, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 24, 30, 40, 55, 75, 105, 150, 220, 330, 512, 800, 1300, 2200, 3500, 4498]

    for idx, n in enumerate(n_list):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 55.0:
            break

        if n <= 12:
            time_for = 110.0
        elif n <= 30:
            time_for = 85.0
        elif n <= 100:
            time_for = 70.0
        elif n <= 500:
            time_for = 70.0
        else:
            time_for = 95.0

        time_for = min(time_for, max(0.0, deadline - now - 60.0))
        if time_for < 18.0:
            break
        stage_deadline = now + time_for

        limit = 8 if n > 500 else 14 if n > 100 else 22
        starts = make_starts(n, rng, limit)

        local_a = None
        local_score = np.inf
        for st in starts:
            sc = _internal_score(st)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
                local_score = float(sc)
                local_a = st
            update_best(st, tag=f"start{n}")

        if local_a is None:
            continue

        # Global-ish search on very small/medium dimensions.
        if n <= 18 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 25.0:
            maxiter = 900 if n <= 10 else 500
            de_a = run_de(n, seed + n, maxiter=maxiter)
            if de_a is not None:
                update_best(de_a, tag=f"de{n}")
                if _internal_score(de_a) < local_score:
                    local_a = _normalize_pos(de_a)
                    local_score = _internal_score(local_a)

        # Principled LP/Frank-Wolfe-like iterations for small n.
        if n <= 26 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 12.0:
            lp_deadline = min(stage_deadline, time.monotonic() + (55.0 if n <= 18 else 30.0))
            lp_a, lp_sc = run_lp(local_a, lp_deadline, max_iter=70 if n <= 18 else 40)
            update_best(lp_a, tag=f"lp{n}")
            if np.isfinite(lp_sc) and lp_sc < local_score:
                local_a = np.asarray(lp_a, dtype=float)
                local_score = float(lp_sc)

        # Smoothed max / L-BFGS refinement for moderate n.
        if n <= 160 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 12.0:
            base = local_a if local_a is not None else _normalize_pos(starts[0])
            u = np.log(base + 1e-12)
            u = u - np.mean(u)
            if n <= 18:
                betas = [12.0 * n, 24.0 * n, 48.0 * n, 96.0 * n]
                mi = 180
            elif n <= 60:
                betas = [10.0 * n, 22.0 * n, 44.0 * n]
                mi = 110
            else:
                betas = [8.0 * n, 18.0 * n, 36.0 * n]
                mi = 60
            sm_deadline = min(stage_deadline, time.monotonic() + (55.0 if n <= 30 else 35.0))
            sm_a, sm_sc = run_smooth_optimize(u, betas, sm_deadline, maxiter_stage=mi)
            update_best(sm_a, tag=f"smooth{n}")
            if np.isfinite(sm_sc) and sm_sc < local_score:
                local_a = np.asarray(sm_a, dtype=float)
                local_score = float(sm_sc)

        # Multiplicative projected subgradient for all sizes.
        if time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 6.0:
            if n <= 18:
                mult_iter = 6000
                stagesm = (10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 80.0, 160.0)
            elif n <= 100:
                mult_iter = 3500
                stagesm = (10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 80.0)
            elif n <= 1000:
                mult_iter = 1200
                stagesm = (10.0, 25.0, 60.0)
            else:
                mult_iter = 450
                stagesm = (10.0, 30.0)
            rm_deadline = min(stage_deadline, time.monotonic() + (45.0 if n <= 50 else 30.0))
            rm_a, rm_sc = run_multiplicative(local_a, rm_deadline, stages=stagesm, max_iter=mult_iter)
            update_best(rm_a, tag=f"mult{n}")
            if np.isfinite(rm_sc) and rm_sc < local_score:
                local_a = np.asarray(rm_a, dtype=float)
                local_score = float(rm_sc)

    # Focused second pass on the most promising sizes if time remains.
    focus = [7, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 28, 40, 60, 90, 140, 220, 380, 700, 1200, 2200, 4498]
    for n in focus:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 80.0:
            break

        if n > 2500:
            time_for = 80.0
        elif n > 200:
            time_for = 55.0
        elif n > 60:
            time_for = 45.0
        else:
            time_for = 70.0
        time_for = min(time_for, max(0.0, deadline - now - 80.0))
        if time_for < 20.0:
            break
        stage_deadline = now + time_for

        starts = make_starts(n, rng, 10 if n > 500 else 20)
        local_a = None
        local_score = np.inf
        for st in starts:
            sc = _internal_score(st)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
                local_score = float(sc)
                local_a = st
            update_best(st, tag=f"focus{n}")

        if local_a is None:
            continue

        if n <= 16 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 30.0:
            de_a = run_de(n, seed + 1000 + n, maxiter=700 if n <= 10 else 400)
            if de_a is not None:
                update_best(de_a, tag=f"focusde{n}")
                if _internal_score(de_a) < local_score:
                    local_a = _normalize_pos(de_a)
                    local_score = _internal_score(local_a)

        if n <= 26 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 15.0:
            lp_deadline = min(stage_deadline, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
            lp_a, lp_sc = run_lp(local_a, lp_deadline, max_iter=50 if n <= 18 else 30)
            update_best(lp_a, tag=f"focuslp{n}")
            if np.isfinite(lp_sc) and lp_sc < local_score:
                local_a = np.asarray(lp_a, dtype=float)
                local_score = float(lp_sc)

        if n <= 160 and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 12.0:
            base = local_a
            u = np.log(base + 1e-12)
            u = u - np.mean(u)
            if n <= 20:
                betas = [16.0 * n, 32.0 * n, 64.0 * n]
                mi = 140
            elif n <= 80:
                betas = [10.0 * n, 24.0 * n]
                mi = 80
            else:
                betas = [8.0 * n, 18.0 * n]
                mi = 45
            sm_deadline = min(stage_deadline, time.monotonic() + (45.0 if n <= 30 else 30.0))
            sm_a, sm_sc = run_smooth_optimize(u, betas, sm_deadline, maxiter_stage=mi)
            update_best(sm_a, tag=f"focussmooth{n}")
            if np.isfinite(sm_sc) and sm_sc < local_score:
                local_a = np.asarray(sm_a, dtype=float)
                local_score = float(sm_sc)

        if time.monotonic() < stage_deadline - 4.0:
            if n <= 50:
                mult_iter = 4000
            elif n <= 300:
                mult_iter = 1500
            else:
                mult_iter = 500
            rm_deadline = min(stage_deadline, time.monotonic() + (35.0 if n <= 100 else 25.0))
            rm_a, rm_sc = run_multiplicative(
                local_a,
                rm_deadline,
                stages=(12.0, 30.0, 70.0),
                max_iter=mult_iter,
            )
            update_best(rm_a, tag=f"focusmult{n}")

    # Final polishing around the best sequence found.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        base = _normalize_pos(best_seq)
        n = int(base.size)
        polish_deadline = deadline - 40.0

        for rep in range(8):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > polish_deadline - 25.0:
                break
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=n)
            cand = base * np.exp(noise)
            cand = _normalize_pos(cand)
            # Sometimes mirror/symmetrize, sometimes not.
            if rep % 3 == 2:
                cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
                cand = _normalize_pos(cand)

            if n <= 100:
                u = np.log(cand + 1e-12)
                u = u - np.mean(u)
                betas = [10.0 * n, 25.0 * n, 50.0 * n][:3]
                sub = min(polish_deadline, now + 22.0)
                a2, sc2 = run_smooth_optimize(u, betas, sub, maxiter_stage=50)
                update_best(a2, tag=f"pol{i//0}{rep}")

            sub = min(polish_deadline, now + 25.0)
            a3, sc3 = run_multiplicative(
                cand,
                sub,
                stages=(15.0, 35.0, 80.0),
                max_iter=300 if n > 100 else 900,
            )
            update_best(a3, tag=f"poly{rep}")

    if best_seq is None:
        # A valid fallback.  The search above should normally replace this.
        best_seq = _normalize_pos(np.array([1.0, 1.0]))

    best_seq = _normalize_pos(np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=float))
    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_seq]

    # Authoritative check and final print.
    try:
        final_official = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        final_official = float("nan")

    print("FINAL internal score:", best_score)
    print("FINAL official score:", final_official)
    print("FINAL length:", len(final_list))

    # If the official evaluator is dramatically worse (should not happen for
    # normalized positive sequences), try a safe rescaling before returning.
    if not np.isfinite(final_official) or final_official > best_score + 1e-6:
        scaled = best_seq * 100.0
        mx = float(np.max(scaled))
        if mx > 0.0 and np.isfinite(mx):
            scaled = scaled * (100.0 / mx)
        final_list = [float(x) for x in scaled]
        try:
            final_official = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        except Exception:
            final_official = float("nan")
        print("FINAL rescaled official score:", final_official)

    return final_list
```
